## 15. 剩余 8 个验收阶段（2026-09-27）

负责人要求将剩余工作具体归并为 8 个 CP，替代此前 18–24 个的粗估。**制定本节时 CP-1–CP-9 已验收，CP-10–CP-17 均未开始；CP-10 的后续授权和实施见第 16 节。** 本节只重排计划，不改变 #356 / v0.2.0 范围，不改 #341/#351/#352 的 UI 归属。

划分原则：每个 CP 交付一个可演示、可恢复的功能组合，其协议、实现、负向测试和恢复测试在该 CP 内完成；不再把每个 helper/schema/reader 单独交给负责人验收。8 是负责人验收次数，不是代码提交数或内部检查次数，也不代表工作量缩成原来的三分之一。M0 未完成的合同/可行性证据随所属能力补齐，不能因 CP-9 通过而视为 M0–M2 全部完成。

### CP-10：真正可恢复的图执行

- **交付**：接通 CP-8 runner 与 CP-9 journal，持久化真实 inputs/outputs、选路、节点执行 receipt、artifact 内容及引用；支持暂停、取消、冷重启、执行 guard 与不确定结果。建立 End completion receipt 及根提交接口，不以最后节点输出代替 End。定义父子 continuation/等待/取消协议，为后续真实 child 提供基础。
- **验收演示**：无模型配置的 Start→分支→具可观察执行计数的受控节点→汇合→End，在节点提交前后、选路后和根提交窗口重启；恢复正确路径，已提交节点不重做，未提交且可能已有副作用的操作停在 outcome_unknown。这里受控 handler 用于调度验证，不冒称生产 Tool/Code 已实现。
- **必须测试**：双执行者/CAS 冲突、重复恢复/取消、损坏或跨运行 receipt、过期 guard、附件内容先于 receipt、源文件变化与保留/清理竞态；新旧 checkpoint 错版本拒绝；纯 Workflow 实际运行不初始化模型、不要求 API key。根会话消费者在 CP-11/CP-14 接通；真实嵌套恢复在 CP-14–CP-16 扩展，不能提前标成已测。
- **包/合同**：M0 剩余耐久合同 + M2；BC-002/006/010/011/012，AC-002/006/010/012/014 的基础子集，SEQ-001/004/008/011/013。依赖 CP-1–CP-9。

### CP-11：Kernel Loop、记忆与多 provider Agent

- **交付**：同图多个 Kernel Loop；各自 provider/model/endpoint/credential profile/请求协议与预算；message/context 和严格 JSON 输出；根历史默认可读、节点私有轮次隔离、显式中间值绑定；压缩显式开启，输出预留与实际请求上限一致。接通真实根回复/记忆 End 消费者；保留既有 FYI/BTW/Auto 和多节点 queue 行为。
- **验收演示**：三个不同 provider 的 Kernel Loop 串接，混用大/小窗口与结构化输出；受控后处理改变 End 值，根回复和记忆取 End；中途失败/冷重启不重复已完成模型请求或其工具操作。Code 生产 runner 到 CP-13 后追加真实后处理场景。
- **必须测试**：完整支持 provider/transport 清单的 strict adapter 矩阵及第 11 节要求的真实服务测试；endpoint/凭据/协议/缓存不串；必需输入超限零调用；不支持能力明确失败；坏 JSON 不发布；未知 usage 不记零；请求结果不确定不自动重发；image-only/附件跨 provider、公开 context 不泄露私有历史；插话真实 route/outbox 链回归。现有模型工具 executor 复用，确定性 Tool Call 节点由 CP-12 接入。
- **包/合同**：M3；BC-002/007/008/010/011/012，AC-007/008/010/011/012/014，SEQ-005/006/008/011/012。依赖 CP-10。CP-17 用最终打包组合重验，不能以此推迟本阶段的 provider 正确性测试。

### CP-12：真实 Tool Call、Skill Read 与审批

- **交付**：Tool Call 接既有正式 executor 和持久身份，复用原权限/审批；参数与结果 schema、错误/超时/安全重试规则；Skill Read 在运行时读取正文并固定快照。工具列表/参数 schema、审批事件与结果提供真实 host 接口，让 #352 可联调。
- **验收演示**：同一工具经模型和节点调用遵循同一批准要求；纯 Workflow 的工具节点批准后执行，重启后复用结果；修改 skill 源文件不改变已提交读取，新 run 才重读。
- **必须测试**：批准/拒绝/重复批准、第二次审批、撤销/过期批准、取消/超时、成功后崩溃、输入变更导致批准失效；原工具无 receipt 时不盲重试；失败继续策略不把拒绝/取消/unknown 当成功；无模型凭据可完成纯工具/Skill Workflow，secret 不进入事件。Hook 强制批准在 CP-16 加入。
- **包/合同**：M0 独立工具 subject 验证 + M4；BC-002/006/007/011，AC-002/006/007/012；SEQ-004/005 及 SEQ-002 非 Hook 部分。依赖 CP-10/CP-11 的稳定执行接口。

### CP-13：Code 节点、Python 环境与独立运行

- **交付**：真实 Python 子进程 JSON 输入输出，独立日志通道；内置 Python 与用户 venv profile，环境/代码指纹固定；timeout/cancel/进程树退出/结果限额；artifact staging copy。提供无 Electron/PuPu UI 的 Python 加载、验证和执行入口，工具由显式 host 注入。Python 项目导出器仍是另票，不偷偷并入 #356。
- **验收演示**：真实 venv 跑 Start→Tool/Skill→Code→End；PuPu 打包提供的解释器执行相同协议；独立 Python 环境运行一份 Skeleton；模型→真实 Code→End 的结果正确。用户代码不因 Workflow 的平台 LLM 限制而被强制网络/SDK 封禁，不自动安装依赖。
- **必须测试**：损坏/缺失环境、非 JSON/错身份/超限输出、stdout 日志污染、依赖缺失、进程崩溃/外部副作用结果未知、超时/取消无遗留子进程、源文件变化不改变在途结果、staging 修改不改原件、权限/内容校验。必须有支持平台的真实 bundled Python 可行性与执行证据；不能用开发机 Python 代替 frozen sidecar 或安装包解释器。
- **包/合同**：M0 打包 Python 验证 + M6；BC-002/003/006/012，AC-002/003/006/012/014，SEQ-004/011。依赖 CP-10/CP-12；模型组合复用 CP-11。

### CP-14：Skeleton 存储、迁移与 Workflow 复用

- **交付**：PuPu loader/store/routes/preload API；草稿保存+诊断、Run/Publish 严格校验；Workflow 创建/发布版本/固定引用、Workflow→Workflow 调用与父子恢复。启用可恢复迁移：旧 recipe/旧 Skeleton 模板/Soul 的适用转换；默认 Agent、旧聊天选择、copy 新 ID、rename/import、共享保存 CAS；归档保留已有引用，物理删除保护。Agent 没有用户发布版本，但每次运行固定 snapshot。
- **验收演示**：真实旧聊天迁移后仍运行所选 Agent；独立 Workflow 保存发布，再被 Agent/更复杂 Workflow 引用；发布新版本不改变暂停运行；两个编辑窗口冲突可见；错误引用明确报错，绝不改跑 Default。使用真实存储与 API，以及 Claude 可用 UI 的往返。
- **必须测试**：真实旧文件/默认 seeds 的有效行为、迁移每一步崩溃与重复迁移、并发编辑源、同名跨来源、缺引用/未知格式、共享 save 冲突、复制身份、旧 seeder 不复活重复资源；完整依赖快照、父子 IO/作用域、缺资源/循环/递归拒绝、归档/恢复、嵌套 Workflow 等待/取消/冷重启。
- **包/合同**：M5 的存储/迁移/Workflow 部分；BC-001/002/004/006/009/010/011/012，AC-001/002/004/006/009/010/012/014，SEQ-003/007/008/011。依赖 CP-10–CP-13；此阶段不宣称 Subagent Pool 执行已完成。

#### CP-14 implementation handoff（2026-09-28）

负责人在 CP-13 验收后授权开始 CP-14。仍只在 `pupu-356` / `unchain-356` 两个隔离工作树中实施；不 commit、push、PR、切换 active rollout、开始 CP-15 或提前关 #356。CP-11 的真实 provider 请求、CP-13 的安装候选/部署解释器对证仍为 NOT_RUN，不因进入 CP-14 被视为通过。

**资源 wire 与存储事务（BC-001/009，SEQ-007）。** 新产品资源以 Skeleton 根 `id` 为唯一身份，名称只用于显示和兼容查找。PuPu store 使用单一 SQLite 事务保存 resource head、不可变 Workflow revision、固定依赖闭包、legacy source mapping 和 migration stage；Definition JSON 仍是严格 `schema_version: 1` Skeleton。草稿保存先走结构校验及诊断，可保留不完整图；Run/Publish 必须通过完整执行校验。每个 head 返回不透明 `edit_token`，所有更新、rename、archive/restore 和 publish 都要求调用方持有当前 token；冲突返回稳定错误，绝不 last-write-wins。Agent 不生成用户可见 revision；运行入口读取一次并固定定义摘要。Workflow publish 生成不可变 revision ID，引用必须精确 `{workflow_id, revision_id}`，不接受 latest/名称回退。归档阻止新增引用，已发布依赖与运行快照仍可解析；存在资源/版本引用时拒绝物理删除。

**迁移与兼容映射（BC-009）。** 迁移记录为 CLOSED `{source_identity,source_kind,source_sha256,target_id,target_sha256,stage}`，stage 只允许 discovered→written→validated→active。转换调用 Unchain 的严格 `convert_legacy_definition`，新产物再经 Skeleton validator；同一来源/同一 hash 重试幂等，不同 hash 或目标冲突拒绝。legacy recipe、旧 template Skeleton、Soul 源字节保留；source scope/path 与旧名映射到稳定资源 ID。Default/Explore 先由既有 seeder 生成，再通过同一迁移器建立唯一 Agent 资源；重复启动不复制。显式旧名/ID 失效返回 missing/ambiguous，只有调用方确实未选择资源时才允许请求默认 Agent。rename 保持 ID；duplicate 生成新根 ID；import 相同 ID 不同内容冲突。

**Workflow 调用与父子恢复（BC-004/006/011/012，SEQ-003/011）。** `workflow_call` config 为 CLOSED `workflow={workflow_id,revision_id}` 加既有 execution bindings/outputs。发布时对每个引用解析固定 revision，拒绝 missing、归档的新引用、错 kind、循环和深度超限，并把完整传递闭包的 definition digest 固定在 revision receipt。Unchain 官方 Workflow Call handler 以父 node invocation 派生确定 child graph identity，复用同一 execution journal/artifact store但建立独立 call-stack frame；child 只接显式 resolved inputs，只返回其 End outputs。父节点保存 `kind=child` wait 后才能推进；冷进程从 parent wait 和 child journal 继续。父 cancel 传播 child；child failed/cancelled/outcome_unknown 不可伪装成功；parent/child definition、revision、call identity 不一致时拒绝。发布新 revision、编辑草稿或归档资源不改变已创建 child 的固定计划。

**产品 API。** Sidecar 提供 Skeleton list/get/create-or-save/diagnose/run-preflight/publish/rename/duplicate/archive/restore/delete/import/migrate/resolve-version 操作；错误统一为稳定 `code + path + message`。Electron main service、IPC channels、preload bridge 与 renderer `api.unchain` 暴露同一固定操作集合，不提供任意 method/path/url 参数。旧 recipe API 暂保兼容；新 API 不借 recipe 名称偷换身份。跨进程 payload、HTTP status 与 preload 方法均有精确正负向测试。

**CP-14 完成门。** 必须用真实 SQLite/store 和 Flask→Electron service/preload 契约完成：草稿 round-trip 与 strict Run/Publish；双编辑窗口 CAS；Workflow 发布/固定 revision/升级不影响暂停 child；嵌套 wait/cancel/cold resume；缺失/归档/错 kind/循环/递归；rename/copy/import identity；三类 legacy 与 Default/Explore 幂等迁移；源变化、各 migration stage 重试和源文件保留；旧聊天选中项通过 legacy mapping 仍解析原 Agent，显式坏引用不回 Default。侧边栏/画布的最终视觉交互仍由 #341 原实现者消费这些 API；本 CP 只要求真实 bridge 往返可用，不重复重绘 UI。部署 wheel/安装包与 Claude 实机组合证据保留 CP-17。

### CP-15：子 Agent、内嵌图与 handoff

- **交付**：Subagent Pool 引用既有 Agent、内嵌新 Agent 图、legacy inline/template 展开；真实 kind 筛选拒绝 Workflow。共享 Agent 编辑影响后续调用，在途运行仍固定 snapshot；下钻/面包屑所需读取保存与冲突语义；临时 child memory、delegate/handoff/worker 行为保留。
- **验收演示**：引用/内嵌 child 都能运行；child 请求批准并冷恢复；handoff→child End→父 Kernel invocation 完成→Code→父 End，没有额外父模型续写和提前根提交。两个父引用同一 Agent，编辑后新运行生效、旧运行不变。
- **必须测试**：真实 producer kind 到 picker/解析器拒绝链、legacy 转换后等价行为、双窗口共享冲突、父 undo 不撤销已保存 child；同 child 多次调用身份/记忆隔离；跨 provider、错误 child End 类型、审批/取消/失败/重启、循环引用；已有 delegate/handoff/worker 并发回归。对应 after Hook 组合留给 CP-16。
- **包/合同**：M5 的 Subagent 部分；BC-001/004/006/009/010/011/012，AC-001/004/006/009/010/011/012/014，SEQ-003/008/011。依赖 CP-11/CP-14。#341 UI 原实现者继续负责交互，不在此重复重绘。

#### CP-15 implementation handoff（2026-09-29）

负责人已授权开始 CP-15。实施继续限定在 `pupu-356` / `unchain-356` 两个隔离工作树；不 commit、push、PR、切换 active rollout、开始 CP-16 或关闭 #356。CP-11 的真实 provider 证据、CP-13 的安装候选解释器证据和 CP-17 的部署组合证据仍为 NOT_RUN。

**Subagent Pool wire（BC-001/004/009）。** `subagent_pool.config` 为 CLOSED `{subagents}`；每项为 CLOSED `{id,name,description,source,allowed_modes,output_mode,memory_policy,parallel_safe,allowed_tools,disabled_tools}`。`source` 只允许 CLOSED `{kind:"agent_ref",agent_id}` 或 `{kind:"embedded",document}`。引用按资源 ID 解析且实际目标必须为 Agent；不得按后缀、旧字段名或显示名猜类型。内嵌 `document` 必须是完整、可执行的 Agent Skeleton。`allowed_modes` 只能包含不重复的 `delegate|handoff|worker`；默认产品迁移值在 producer 明确写入，不由 consumer 静默补齐。`memory_policy` 为 `ephemeral|scoped_persistent`，0.2.0 新建项默认由 producer 写 `ephemeral`。未知 key/kind/mode、Workflow、缺失/归档引用和空 Pool 条目均拒绝。

**固定引用与递归（BC-004/006/009/011）。** PuPu Run preflight 在任何模型调用前解析整棵 Agent 引用树，固定每个资源的 document、definition digest 和当时 edit token；同一次运行后续 child invocation 只消费该快照。新运行重新解析 head，因此共享 Agent 编辑只影响新运行。解析使用资源 ID 栈拒绝直接/间接循环，并同时检查内嵌图；最大子图深度沿用现有 subagent policy 6。资源归档阻止新运行，但不改变已经固定的运行 manifest。manifest 是运行输入，不回写用户草稿，也不创造 Agent 发布版本。

**Kernel 与现有 subagent runtime（BC-010/011/012，SEQ-008/011）。** 每个 attach 到 Kernel 节点的 Pool 在该节点构造 `SubagentModule`，模板的 child adapter 运行固定 `PreparedAgentPlan`，复用父 execution journal/content store，但使用由父 node invocation、tool call、child ordinal 派生的 graph/attempt identity和独立临时 memory namespace。delegate/handoff/worker 继续使用既有 tool 名称、并发 policy、取消和 communication state；不另造第二套模型子 Agent 调度器。handoff 成功只令当前 Kernel Agent 返回 completed；Skeleton Kernel handler把 child End 严格映射为当前节点的 `message/context`，保存 Kernel result 后父 DAG继续 Code/End。只有父 End 可提交根回复；不得额外调用父模型续写。

**等待与失败序列。** child interaction 以 child call-stack/attempt 身份进入父图的 durable wait；恢复必须先验证 Pool snapshot、tool call、child graph、node invocation和 interaction ID，再恢复 child，不重跑父 Kernel 的已完成 provider/tool步骤。child failed/cancelled/outcome_unknown、错误 End 类型或损坏 receipt向上传播，不能伪装 tool success。父取消传播到活跃 child；重复批准和旧窗口回复拒绝。CP-15必须保留直接 legacy Agent terminal handoff 原行为，只有 Skeleton graph host使用“结束当前 Kernel invocation”语义。

**迁移与编辑边界。** legacy `recipe_ref` 只有在解析到 Agent 后才能变为 `agent_ref`；legacy inline/template 先经现有严格 converter 生成完整 Agent document，再作为 embedded 或独立 Agent保存。不能把 Workflow 展开成 subagent。引用 Agent 的下钻读写独立资源并使用其 edit token；embedded 下钻读写父草稿并使用父 token。父画布 undo/dirty 不撤销已经成功保存的独立 child 资源；双窗口冲突返回既有 `edit_conflict`。最终面包屑和画布交互仍由 #341 消费这些合同，本 CP 不重画 UI。

**完成门。** 使用真实 SQLite/resource store、真实 Skeleton durable runner和现有 SubagentModule，覆盖引用/内嵌 child、共享资源新旧运行快照、kind 拒绝、六层深度/循环、同一 child 多次 identity与 memory 隔离、delegate/handoff/worker并发回归、跨 provider、错误 End、审批/拒绝/取消/失败/冷重启。handoff组合必须观测 child End→父 Kernel result→Code→父 End 的持久顺序，并断言没有额外父 provider request、没有提前 root completion。after Hook不在本阶段实现。

### CP-16：Hooks 完整调用链

- **交付**：事件匹配/指定工具匹配，Hook 绑定非 LLM Workflow，before/after 顺序与决策；参数修改后按最终参数审批，多个要求累积；child Tool Call 审批与父工具审批；after 每阶段持久化，完成前结果不可消费。
- **验收演示**：before 修改参数并要求批准，Hook 子工具先请求批准，父工具再请求批准；after H1 脱敏→H2 格式化。逐阶段断电后继续，工具/H1 不重跑；next model、summary、公开 context、binding/artifact 只见最终结果；审计仍保留原文。
- **必须测试**：非法 decision/参数、未知事件、重复 hook/重复批准、拒绝/取消/unknown、第二阶段失败、工具不继承不适用 Hook、Workflow 禁平台 LLM 的间接路径；raw sentinel 在全部真实消费者消失；handoff 的适用 after Hook；Code/Tool/嵌套 Workflow Hook 恢复；工具结果已成功时，后处理配额失败不改判工具结果未知。
- **包/合同**：M7；BC-004/005/006/007/008/010/011/012，AC-004/005/006/007/010/011/012/013/014，SEQ-002/003/009/010/011。依赖 CP-12–CP-15。

### CP-17：完整产品联调与交付证据

- **交付**：将前述已完成能力与 #341/#351/#352 的真实 UI 合验；导入/编辑/草稿诊断/运行/状态/usage/审批/取消/恢复/下钻完整流程；实际 runtime manifest 准入和旧入口兼容。所有 BC-001–012、AC-001–014、SEQ-001–014 汇总核对，不能以单元测试数量代替覆盖。
- **验收演示**：同一保留的 PuPu candidate + 一次构建后复用的 unchain wheel，运行纯 Workflow、混合 provider Agent、嵌套 child、before/after Hook，并跨重启完成；独立 Python 无 UI 运行同一 Skeleton；支持平台安装包 smoke。
- **必须测试**：全回归、真实 provider/代表性混合链在最终产物重验、实际 wheel 来源与 manifest/digest、旧聊天/默认 Agent/迁移切换、过旧/错误 runtime 拒绝、审批重入及所有严重故障窗口。重建 wheel 或修改相关候选后，重跑受影响证据。缺凭据/服务/平台/Claude UI 时明确 NOT_RUN，该项未完成，不能称 CP-17 或 ticket 已完成。
- **包/合同**：M8；所有适用 BC/AC/SEQ 的部署产物对证，依赖 CP-10–CP-16 及兄弟 UI 交付。通过仅代表实现和验证准备齐全；PR、feature audit、合并与关票按用户后续指令及 issue-start-ticket 流程执行。

### 阶段节奏、风险与覆盖核对

1. 每次负责人授权一个 CP。开始时强模型确认具体 API/状态转移、GitNexus impact、精确文件范围与负向测试，再将已确定的实现单元交给 Terra；恢复、provider 和迁移边界保留强模型审查，不能整包交给弱模型自由设计。
2. 每个 CP 内按“合同/失败序列 → 实现和真实适配 → 测试及强审查”设内部检查点；这些不是新增负责人 CP，也不命名为 CP-10A/10B。内部修复仍属本 CP；结束报告后停下来，由负责人验收并决定下一步。
3. CP-10、CP-11、CP-14、CP-16 风险较高、可能耗时较长；8 阶段是减少验收切换，不是小改动承诺。提前在 CP-10 核对工具 subject/打包解释器约束，CP-11 核对真实 provider 访问条件，避免到最终验收才暴露不可行项。若发现必须改变范围/阶段的真实阻碍，报告证据；不暗增 CP、不削功能、不把未测改为通过。
4. 从 CP-10 起冻结给 Claude 消费的真实 fixtures/事件/诊断；每阶段完成相关 host 适配及契约测试。CP-12 交工具/Skill，CP-13 交 Code，CP-14 交资源/迁移，CP-15 交 subagent，CP-16 交 Hook；有对应 UI 即联调。CP-17 是组合和安装产物验收，不是首次接线。早期能力使用测试/开发入口，不提前宣布 runtime 支持未完成能力或开启 active rollout。
5. 新增或改动持久/进程/provider 合同，先更新对应 BC/SEQ/AC 和版本策略；不能无条件复用 CP-7/CP-9 旧格式、宽松接收新增状态。保留旧 reader 或明确拒绝不兼容恢复，完成旧证据回归。
6. 覆盖归属：定义/绑定/分支在 CP-1–CP-8 已有静态证据，CP-10/CP-14/CP-17 补真实消费；恢复与 End 归 CP-10/CP-11；模型与 memory 归 CP-11；工具/Skill 归 CP-12；Code/独立 Python 归 CP-13；迁移/Workflow 复用归 CP-14；子 Agent 归 CP-15；Hooks 归 CP-16；最终混合 provider/附件/嵌套/打包组合归 CP-17。排他 DAG 边界不变，已有 subagent 并发保留。

本次仅更新此计划和 #356 进度；没有运行新测试、修改产品代码、启动 CP-10、commit、push、PR 或关票。

## 16. CP-10 implementation handoff — durable Workflow execution (2026-09-27)

Owner authorized CP-10 after the eight-CP roadmap. Work only in the recorded two clones. No CP-11, product store cutover, model/tool/Code production handler, commit/push/PR/rollout. CP-9 remains accepted for descriptor persistence, not active execution. Current stop: CP-10 implementation, then owner acceptance.

**Research and impact.** Clone-local GitNexus index is at Unchain base 0b2ad609; `run_workflow`, `save_workflow_recovery`, proposed `DurableWorkflowRunner`, `WorkflowExecutionStore`, and `validate_execution_state` return UNKNOWN/not found. Source search finds the existing two APIs only in isolated Skeleton exports/tests and no production caller for the new APIs. Reuse `BoundExecutionJournal.append_with_artifacts` and the existing SQLite bound artifact repository without modifying their transaction semantics. The transaction checks exact-operation replay before precondition, then precondition, artifact rows/content, and event. Consequently duplicate start receipts must NEVER authorize a second dispatch. Existing file-based ExecutionGuard is a different store: its assertion is not an atomic SQLite fence. CP-10 uses graph-local revision CAS and persisted per-node owner tokens inside the journal transaction; no claim of atomic fencing across separate stores or exactly-once external effects.

**Version decision / BC-006/011 extension.** Keep CP-7/CP-9 v1 readers and records unchanged. Their active variants cannot represent ready/running/received/cancelled, and outcome_unknown is intentionally terminal. Introduce event `skeleton.workflow_execution.saved`, payload schema `unchain.skeleton_workflow_execution_journal.v2`, state schema `unchain.skeleton_execution_state.v2`. A v1-only graph is explicitly refused by the new runner, never silently restarted or converted without missing content receipts. A v2 record does not masquerade as a v1 recovery descriptor. This is a versioned evolution using the same journal atomic primitives, not a second persistence backend.

**Canonical state (CLOSED).** Exact root fields: `schema, graph_id, attempt, definition_digest, call_stack, inputs_ref, attachments, completed, active, status, paused, error, root_committed`. `attempt` is exact journal AttemptRef.to_dict(); call_stack uses CP-7 frame shape. Current frame must match graph/execution/attempt/definition. attachments maps explicit input names to full canonical ArtifactRef.to_dict() descriptors. inputs_ref is the persisted root JSON object. Completed rows are exact `{node_id,node_invocation_id,node_attempt_id,input_ref,result_ref,output_ref,edge_id}`; edge_id is null only at End. Active is null or `{node_id,node_invocation_id,node_attempt_id,input_ref,owner_token,result_ref,wait}`. result_ref is null until received; wait is null or `{kind,id,request_ref}` with kind interaction|child. Node identities are deterministic from graph attempt + node ID; owner token identifies one dispatch. Error is null or `{code,path}` with no exception text or values. Status is ready|running|waiting|result_received|outcome_unknown|failed|cancelled|completed. paused is an orthogonal flag; root_committed is false until an End-only root completion acknowledgement. No arbitrary new fields, inferred provider settings, or implicit retries.

**Content / BC-012.** Root inputs, each resolved input, raw handler result, mapped output, and wait request are canonical JSON byte artifacts. Binary attachments are byte artifacts, snapshotted before the run is admitted. All use existing execution-bound content-addressed storage, an empty preview, stable graph-qualified operation keys and full digest/size/media descriptors. Atomic event.resource_refs enumerate references so storage retention can see them. New content becomes durable before its event; read verifies full bytes and scope, never rereads a mutable source path. CP-10 conservatively retains committed artifacts for execution lifetime; it does not introduce a GC that can delete paused-run data. Cross-provider and child grants remain later CPs. Strict JSON uses finite values, bounded depth/size and duplicate-key rejection, preserving Unicode distinctly through canonical UTF-8 surrogatepass bytes/base64 transport; unsupported encoding/resource limits fail explicitly.

**State sequence / SEQ-014 (extends SEQ-001/004/008/011/013).** Create ready with persisted inputs → pure Start/condition commits receipt+chosen edge atomically; before an injected external handler, commit running+input+owner token → assert current token → invoke once → commit raw result as result_received → map/validate output and atomically append completion+edge → next node. End maps inputs and records its receipt; only completed root graphs may append root_committed. Actual PuPu reply/memory consumers remain CP-11/14. Failure before dispatch becomes failed; an exception or crash after running with no raw result becomes outcome_unknown on explicit recovery. A saved raw result is never discarded or used to rerun the handler, including output-mapping failures. Waiting stores request identity and only an explicit matching completion may supply a result. Actual approval/child executors are later CPs.

**Repeat/recovery/control.** Create repeats only with identical inputs/attachments/lineage. Journal save retries exact operations idempotently; runner dispatch requires a newly acquired running transition, not replay. Resume reads and verifies every receipt without reevaluating committed branches. Observing a running node via normal run returns running; an explicit host cold-recovery request fences its token and records outcome_unknown (no time-based auto-takeover). Controls serialize via the same graph CAS. pause prevents the next dispatch, but an already running handler may seal its result; cancel is terminal and retains active uncertainty/receipts. A stale owner cannot commit results or execute downstream nodes. Reset uses a new graph identity; no rollback of external effects. A crash after End or root acknowledgement cannot produce a second completion identity. Foreign attempt/definition/content, corrupted newer events, wrong version, non-prefix transitions, and mismatched waiting IDs fail closed. Never fall back to an older good state.

**Implementation slices and APIs.** Strong parent writes `src/unchain/skeleton/durable_protocol.py` and `durable_execution.py` plus runner integration tests. Terra implements only `durable_store.py` and its persistence tests against the frozen contract; parent reviews before dependent execution tests. Public storage interface:

- `WorkflowExecutionStore(journal, plan, *, graph_id, attempt)`; journal supplies BoundExecutionJournal plus `artifact_id_for` and `read_full_verified` capabilities.
- `prepare_json(value, *, key)` / `prepare_bytes(content, *, media_type, key)` return `(artifact_descriptor_dict, PendingArtifact)` without writes; key is stable within graph.
- `read_json(ref)` / `read_bytes(ref)` strictly verify descriptor, execution scope, size/digest and canonical JSON as applicable.
- `load() -> ExecutionRecord | None`; record has `revision, state, event_id, store_seq`.
- `save(state, *, expected_revision, operation_id, artifacts=()) -> ExecutionRecord`; exact old operation replay returns original record without granting dispatch. New artifacts and state commit atomically. Unknown journal type lacking the capabilities fails, never uses a non-atomic fallback.
- Protocol helpers supplied by parent: `validate_execution_state(state, plan=None)`, `validate_execution_transition(previous, current, plan)`, `execution_artifacts(state)`, `canonical_bytes(value)`, `decode_json(content)`, `node_identity(state,node_id)`. Validator with no plan checks closed self-contained shape/identity before graph filtering; with plan additionally checks topology/path and node-type constraints. Store validates all matching event headers and every selected graph revision/transition. Event payload exact fields: `schema,graph_key,revision,previous_event_id,encoded_state,state_sha256`; graph key SHA-256 exact graph ID UTF-8 surrogatepass. Operation ID `skeleton.execution.<graph_key>.<sha256(caller operation id)>`; event ID `event.<operation_id>`; operation domain `skeleton.workflow_execution.saved`. Encoded state uses canonical JSON bytes + strict base64. Each event binds its full attempt, exact operation digest, resource_refs and predecessor. Capacity checked before writing, never create an unreadable oversized snapshot.

**Tests and supervision.** PARTIALLY SUITABLE: parent owns state transitions, effect fencing and runner design; Terra (`gpt-5.6-terra`) owns settled storage adapter/tests only. Storage tests use actual SQLite, fresh processes, CAS races, transaction failures, exact replay after progress, foreign/corrupt event identities, resource scope, bad/unknown fields, output content tampering and v1 rejection. Runner tests prove branch/rejoin order, each crash window, save-before-effect, outcome_unknown, pause/resume/cancel while handler runs, wrong waiting identity, End-only projection, repeat generation/graph isolation, attachment snapshot stability and lost replies. Run targeted Skeleton + journal/artifact regression tests, then full Unchain suite if required by integration reach. Real SQLite imports existing SDK modules transitively; verify no model/client construction or API-key access, while new explicit Skeleton modules remain provider-import-free. Do not claim import isolation for the old concrete persistence package. No sidecar is rewired, so production sidecar restart and exact deployed artifact-pair evidence remain NOT_RUN.

At each internal boundary record continue/correct/takeover. No internal slice is another owner CP. Stop after complete CP-10 implementation and strong development review; do not label owner acceptance PASS in advance.

**CP-10 internal review corrections.** The initial runner/storage review required corrections before continuing: canonical ArtifactRef normalization is refused; cold loading validates the initial transition as well as subsequent ones; event resource references must exactly match encoded state, including foreign headers; artifact slots are bound to graph-qualified operation IDs, not merely the same execution. `complete_wait(wait_id, result, *, node_invocation_id)` requires the exact node invocation so a delayed response cannot resolve a later node with a reused wait label. A pause/resume race retries receipt persistence only while the same running token still owns the node; cancellation/recovery still fence it. Malformed handler wait variants are classified as outcome_unknown, not an uncaught native type error. Real SQLite probes reproduced the wait-response and pause race before correction; retained tests cover them. These are fixes inside CP-10, not new owner checkpoints.

**Development consumer entry (explicit imports).** `unchain.skeleton.durable_execution` exports `DurableWorkflowRunner`, `DurableWorkflowResult`, `DurableNodeContext`, `WorkflowNodeWaiting`; the existing `WorkflowNodeCompleted` is unchanged. Construct a real execution-bound journal and `WorkflowExecutionStore` as above, then `runner = DurableWorkflowRunner(store, handlers={"code": controlled_handler})`. `create({"flag": True})` persists an initial run; `run(max_steps=...)`, `inspect()`, `pause()`, `resume()`, `cancel()` and explicit `recover()` operate on the same graph identity. `recover()` only fences an interrupted running intent as unknown; it does not replay it. `complete_wait(..., node_invocation_id=...)` accepts a trusted host's matched result; it is not a production approval-policy endpoint. A completed result exposes End outputs and a stable completion ID; `commit_root()` records its idempotent root acknowledgement, with actual PuPu chat/memory projection still pending. An injected handler receives invocation identity, `assert_current()` and scoped `read_attachment(name)` access. JSON/path diagnostics use the existing structured error conventions. Original CP-8/CP-9 imports and behavior are unchanged; no new package-level eager persistence/provider imports are added.

### CP-10 implementation result — ready for owner acceptance (2026-09-27)

Implemented three explicit modules (`durable_protocol.py`, `durable_store.py`, `durable_execution.py`) and two test files. Real SQLite now holds root inputs, binary attachment snapshots, per-node input/raw-result/export artifacts, selected edges, execution intent/owner token, waiting state and End/root acknowledgement. The runner advances one exclusive path; pure nodes and their selected edges commit together. A durable raw result precedes mapping, so a mapping failure cannot repeat the handler. A lost host with only a running intent stops at outcome_unknown; observing a running owner does not implicitly take over. Pause/resume/cancel and result persistence use the same graph revision CAS. Host wait resolution is scoped to node invocation, and parent/child call-stack metadata is fixed and validated; actual nested executors remain later work.

Terra implemented the bounded storage slice. Internal strong review requested the corrections recorded above plus strict v1 header admission before filtering; all were addressed. The independent strong reviewer reran real SQLite probes for stale wait responses, pause races while sealing both result and wait receipts, malformed waits, and cancellation/recovery during receipt retry. It found no further blocking runner/protocol issue, and its final read-only storage pass likewise found no further blocker. This is development supervision, not owner acceptance or feature audit.

**Evidence:**

- Original CP-1–CP-9 + atomic journal regression: `PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_skeleton_io.py tests/test_skeleton_conditions.py tests/test_skeleton_schema.py tests/test_skeleton_validation.py tests/test_skeleton_legacy.py tests/test_skeleton_preparation.py tests/test_skeleton_recovery.py tests/test_skeleton_execution.py tests/test_skeleton_recovery_journal.py tests/context_v2/test_journal_atomic_append.py` — **199 passed in 4.09s**.
- Durable runner integration: `PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_skeleton_durable_execution.py -o faulthandler_timeout=30` — **38 passed in 43.50s**. One earlier integration process was terminated with exit 143; it is not counted as a pass. The clean rerun is the cited result.
- Storage/recovery/atomic journal: `PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_skeleton_durable_store.py tests/test_skeleton_recovery_journal.py tests/context_v2/test_journal_atomic_append.py` — **75 passed in 3.58s**. These overlap the prior regression; counts are not additive.
- Stable-source whole repository: `PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q` — **3,963 passed, 16 skipped, 5 xfailed in 125.02s**. This includes 56 new CP-10 cases. No failing test is waived.
- Retained recovery evidence includes real fresh-process continuation from a received result; a child process fsyncs one external-effect marker then calls `os._exit(17)`, and the next host records unknown rather than calling it again; two concurrent runners race on one intent and only one dispatches; cancellation/recovery fence late result writes; End differs from the last handler's value and root acknowledgement survives a lost response.
- Admission evidence includes exact schema/unknown-field/operation/event identities before filtering, valid foreign-graph coexistence, v1/v2 incompatibility, same-execution cross-graph artifact borrowing rejection, invalid initial transitions, missing/corrupt content, actual atomic rollback after artifact rows, and snapshot retention after source-file deletion. Schema and scope failures do not fall back to older progress.
- Actual red evidence: pause-between-guard-and-CAS regression failed with JournalConflictError before the receipt-only retry; stale wait response was accepted by the independent pre-fix SQLite probe and is refused after node binding. Terra's isolated in-memory removal of admission checks made an invalid running/no-active state durably append, failing its negative assertion; checks were restored before the passing suites. No on-disk source mutation or stale pycache is used for this proof.
- Fresh-process import guard proves the new explicit modules do not import model/provider packages. Actual SQLite execution is tested with model constructors and API-key reads forbidden; no model client or key is needed. The existing concrete SQLite package still imports SDK definitions, as disclosed above.
- Whitespace check passed on the five new files. Existing tracked/staged source diff remains empty; all work is isolated new files, not main-tree changes. CP-9 source/test hashes still match its accepted evidence.

**Scope/stop:** BC-006/010/011/012 and SEQ-014 have the CP-10 library-level evidence above; later provider/production Tool/Skill/Code/actual child/Hook executors, cross-provider attachments, PuPu reply/memory consumers and exact installed artifact-pair evidence remain PENDING/NOT_RUN in their assigned CPs. Artifact retention is conservative (no new GC); parent/child waits are protocol foundations, not a claim of production subagent recovery. No PuPu sidecar was rewired or restarted; fresh test interpreters exercised the changed Python. No CP-11, commit, push, PR, feature audit, rollout or closure. Stop for owner acceptance.

### CP-10 owner-requested independent acceptance — PASS (2026-09-27)

The owner's subsequent “验收” triggered a fresh read-only review, not another implementation pass. Verdict: **PASS for the frozen CP-10 library scope; no blocking finding**. Source and retained tests were not modified. All five source/test SHA-256 values still match the implementation evidence above. GitNexus query/context cannot resolve the new untracked modules; its result is UNKNOWN, corroborated with source inspection and live SQLite probes, not treated as an all-clear.

Fresh verification in `/Users/red/Desktop/GITRepo/unchain-356`:

- `PYTHONPATH=src .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q tests/test_skeleton*.py tests/context_v2/test_journal_atomic_append.py -o faulthandler_timeout=30` — **255 passed in 48.68s**. This covers CP-1–CP-10 and atomic journal regressions, including real process loss and cold continuation tests.
- Independent storage review and real-SQLite probes: 70 malformed state replacements rejected with structured errors; cold reads rejected unknown schema, revision gaps, wrong predecessor/attempt, premature root acknowledgement, rewritten branch receipts and invocation corruption. Digest, length, media type, preview and corrupt object bytes failed closed. Binary content survived a cold read; exact old create replay returned its original receipt without reverting current progress. Two independent connections at one revision produced one winner and one CAS conflict. Parent reran the probe successfully. Preserved probe: `/Users/red/.codex/artifacts/pupu-356/cp10-acceptance/storage_probe.py`.
- Additional runner probe: two concurrent responses to the same wait admit exactly one; a result received while paused stays paused and dispatches no next handler; resume consumes that receipt without repeating the prior handler; cancellation after the next raw receipt prevents End and root acknowledgement. Passed against the real store using separate runner instances.
- The previously recorded full-suite **3,963 passed, 16 skipped, 5 xfailed** is unchanged-source development evidence, not falsely represented as a new full-suite run in this acceptance turn.

This acceptance establishes durable exclusive-path execution, receipt/content integrity, CAS ownership, pause/cancel/recovery behavior, matched waits and End-only completion interfaces. It does not establish real production Code/tool/Skill/child/Hook handlers, model/provider execution, PuPu reply/memory projection or installed artifact-pair integration. Those remain assigned to CP-11–CP-17. No production sidecar changed or needed a restart; fresh interpreters supplied the Python verification. The prior local backup remains a snapshot of implementation before this acceptance note, not a remote delivery.

**Stop:** CP-10 accepted; CP-11 (Kernel Loop, memory and mixed-provider Agent execution) not started. Seven checkpoints remain. Issue #356 stays open/In Progress; no product edits, commit, push, PR, feature audit, rollout or closure in this turn.

## 17. CP-11 implementation handoff (2026-09-27, in progress)

The owner authorized the next stage after CP-10 PASS. CP-11 scope remains section 15: actual Kernel Loop nodes, model/profile isolation, context budgets, strict structured output, root history with private node turns, End-only PuPu root completion, and interjection compatibility. No CP-12, UI changes, commit/push/PR/rollout. CP-10 evidence remains historical, not evidence for modified CP-11 modules.

Research binds the two existing ticket clones at the previously recorded bases. Both clone-local GitNexus indexes describe their base commits; new Skeleton symbols are unindexed/UNKNOWN and require source corroboration. Existing reuse points include Agent/PreparedAgent/KernelLoop, ContextRuntime's durable provider/tool boundaries, provider prepared-wire adapters, and PuPu's canonical root completion/Memory V2 factory. Existing Anthropic ResponseFormat is prompt-only and cannot be advertised as native strict output. Existing PuPu graph completion reads the last model step and cannot be reused unchanged as an End consumer. Preserve the old direct/recipe runtime while adding the explicit Skeleton path.

**Delegation assessment: partially suitable.** Durable node continuation, graph schema evolution, root completion and integration remain with the strong parent until concrete contracts are settled. The pure structured-output validator is suitable for Terra: its contract and negative tests do not depend on those decisions. Internal slice checkpoints are development supervision, not additional owner CPs.

**Bounded Terra slice CP11-A (frozen):** work only in `/Users/red/Desktop/GITRepo/unchain-356`, new `src/unchain/skeleton/kernel_output.py` and `tests/test_skeleton_kernel_output.py`. No existing source/provider files, dependency manifests or plan edits. Public functions: `validate_output_schema(schema) -> detached dict`, and `parse_kernel_output(text, schema) -> detached dict`. Raise SkeletonValidationError with content-free diagnostics, never include generated values. The schema must be a JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 object with a root `type: object`; reserve root property names message/context. Use the installed jsonschema package (parent owns declaring the dependency); allow only a documented bounded subset: `$schema`, `$defs`, `$ref`, `title`, `description`, `type`, `properties`, `required`, `additionalProperties` (bool only), `items`, `enum`, `const`, `anyOf`, `minItems`, `maxItems`, `minLength`, `maxLength`, `minimum`, `maximum`. Local references may resolve only under `#/$defs/`; no remote ref, anchors, dynamic refs, unknown validation keywords or automatic repair/coercion. Permit nullable type arrays. Validate schema syntax and reference targets before use; ensure nested schema objects do not accidentally admit unsupported keywords. Reuse Skeleton JSON resource limits; reject duplicate JSON keys, NaN/Infinity, non-object final JSON, bad types/enum/required/additional properties and reserved output collisions. `$schema` if present must equal `https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema`. Reject reference cycles rather than risking unbounded validator recursion. Preserve Unicode values and explicit nulls. No SDK import, no model call, no prompt-only fallback. Worker must run upstream impact for new symbols, corroborate UNKNOWN, inspect CP1 error conventions and report actual test evidence. Test with `.venv/bin/python` and `PYTHONPATH=src`; stop after this slice for parent review.

**Boundary mapping:** CP11-A refines BC-007 / AC-007 (closed supported JSON-schema dialect and strict final-result admission); it cannot independently prove native provider constraints. CP-11 as a whole retains BC-002/007/008/010/011/012, AC-007/008/010/011/012/014 and SEQ-005/006/008/011/012. Further concrete runtime/profile/state contracts will be recorded here before their edits. Live provider permission/configuration is being resolved while local work proceeds; missing required real-service evidence remains NOT_RUN and blocks CP-11 acceptance, not local implementation.

**CP11-B graph/profile contract (parent):** add a distinct `PreparedAgentPlan` and provider-free `prepare_agent_graph`; keep `prepare_workflow`, non-durable Workflow execution and v1 recovery readers rejecting Agents. The durable scheduler may consume either exact prepared type; Agent remains a declared Agent throughout (no conversion into a fake Workflow). The v2 execution receipt shape/status machine stays unchanged: an Agent node uses the existing running/input/raw-result phases. Its complete definition including kind and resolved profile metadata is digested; an old reader rejects the Agent plan, so this is new plan capability admission, not silently widening v1. New node configs use `config.execution={inputs,outputs}` and `config.kernel={schema_version,profile,prompt,max_iterations,output_tokens,history,overflow,json_schema}`. Profile is CLOSED `{id,provider,model,protocol,endpoint,credential_ref,context_window,output_limit,capabilities}` with exact boolean capabilities `{json_schema,images,files}`; it contains no credential bytes. Supported current transports are openai-responses, anthropic, gemini and ollama; brand names are separate from transport. Current custom and shipped providers map to those transports; OpenAI Chat is not currently supported and cannot be claimed. Profile/endpoint/credential reference are immutable definition inputs; host resolution must match them, never use a previous node's factory/key. Node history is root|none; overflow is error|compress, compression requires explicit host implementation and remains opt-in. Output cap is a positive integer bounded by profile output limit and context window. Invalid profile/config/output schemas must fail preflight even on an unselected branch. Pools are attach resources, not executable flow nodes; actual subagent/Hook executors remain later CPs. BC-008/011 and SEQ-006/008 cover this typed-plan admission and fixed-profile lifecycle; preserve CP1–10 negatives and add exact Agent/Workflow/version/profile mismatch tests before dependent integration.

**CP11-C immutable NodeMemoryView (parent, BC-008/010; SEQ-015):** keep the complete contiguous journal; never relabel a filtered prefix as a JournalSnapshot. Persist a CLOSED `unchain.node_memory_view.v1` selector per node attempt, binding the fixed definition digest and exact public root-history event cursors/hashes. The same selector is mounted on both JournalContextRequestFactory and ContextCompileCoordinator; ContextExecutionBundle rejects a mismatched pair. Selection admits only the fixed public root-history receipts plus this node's own attempt events. It excludes siblings before canonical projection, pending-input admission and checkpoint dependency construction. Selection verifies the original complete snapshot and persisted selector identity; missing/changed view or history receipts fail closed. Existing callers default to no selector and retain existing behavior. Same node attempt cannot rebind its history or definition; cold rebuild reuses the original selector. Root history selection rejects child/graph-step records rather than inferring publicness from a model's output. Build receipts remain v1 because the view is immutable before their first trigger; a different view requires a different node attempt, and build IDs include the selector digest. FYI extensions must use the existing matched node attempt's ingress receipts, not mutate the fixed root-history list. The first/second model request, two nodes, wrong scope, replay and cold-rebind negatives will test the real request factory plus coordinator. Impact: request factory LOW (3 direct importers); `_prepare_journal_view` LOW (compile, 7 affected flows); coordinator LOW (3 direct importers); bundle MEDIUM (5 direct importers). Index at base; dynamic PuPu construction is additionally confirmed by source.

**CP11-D native profile binding (bounded Terra slice):** `bind_kernel_profile(config, *, profile_resolver, credential_resolver, client_factories=None)` in new `skeleton/kernel_profile.py` must compare the host-resolved CLOSED profile to the definition's exact profile before credential lookup. Return a frozen binding containing detached config, official exact ModelIO instance, transport provider, native payload, optional ResponseFormat and a credential-free profile digest. Create a new adapter per binding, no global model/default catalog: use explicit per-model payload/capability dictionaries; pin endpoint and disable SDK implicit retries. Public profile brand remains separate from native transport (`anthropic` brand -> AnthropicModelIO; hyperspace/custom/shipped anthropic brands -> HyperspaceModelIO with route-bound endpoint; other protocols use official OpenAI/Gemini/Ollama). ### CP11 owner-requested independent acceptance — FAIL (2026-09-27)

**P1 / CP11-R1: recovery ignores already durable Kernel waiting/completion state.** `DurableWorkflowRunner.recover()` (`skeleton/durable_execution.py:367–388`) consults only `SkeletonKernelHandler.recover_result()`, which requires the extra `skeleton.kernel_result.saved` receipt. If that receipt is absent, it permanently converts a running node to `outcome_unknown` without reconciling the Kernel's durable state.

Two independent crash probes on actual SQLite plus file-backed Kernel checkpoints reproduced the gap:

1. The native Kernel writes a real tool-approval checkpoint and pending interaction, then the process dies before the graph's `waiting` save. A fresh SessionStore reads the checkpoint with the exact active node attempt and tool-approval continuation. Cold `recover()` nevertheless returns `outcome_unknown`; answering the saved approval fails with `Kernel wait is not current`. Initial provider calls = 1; cold calls = 0; tool calls = 0. The approval is durably known, not an unknown external tool outcome.
2. The native Kernel has already persisted `provider.turn_result`, node `final_message`, and node `run_completed`, then the process dies before `_save_completion()` writes the additional handler receipt. A fresh SQLite binding verifies these node receipts, but cold `recover()` still returns terminal `outcome_unknown`. It avoids duplicates but cannot continue an already completed node.

Required correction: reconcile exact, authoritative Kernel state before the unknown fallback. Restore a matched durable approval as waiting and reuse a fully completed node's authoritative result without starting the Agent again. Keep truly incomplete/uncertain provider sends unknown; enforce graph/node/attempt/definition/provider/checkpoint identity and CAS ownership, reject foreign/corrupt/stale evidence, and add both first/second-approval and completed-node receipt-gap crash tests. Do not fix this by rerunning the entire Kernel or creating a fresh attempt.

**Evidence:** retained standalone probe and JSON output are outside both working trees at `/Users/red/.codex/artifacts/pupu-356/cp11-acceptance/recovery_probe.py` and `recovery_probe.json`. It uses strict SDK fakes, actual Kernel/provider execution, and reconstructed durable stores. No live calls or production mutation. All 78 non-document source/test/fixture files match the previous handoff manifest; no product or retained repository test was edited during review.

The previous whole-repository 4,062-pass result was not rerun or presented as fresh evidence.

### CP11-R1 recovery correction — locally fixed, CP11 still not accepted (2026-09-27)

The correction reconciles authoritative evidence in this order before the `outcome_unknown` fallback: an exact `skeleton.kernel_result.saved` receipt, an active durable Kernel interaction checkpoint, or a complete Kernel terminal chain. A recovered interaction must match execution session, graph node attempt, continuation run, interaction/checkpoint/request identities, provider, model and prompt manifest digest; its canonical request becomes the graph's waiting artifact under the existing owner-token CAS. Resume deliberately bypasses pending-wait discovery after the graph has acquired the new resume owner, so the official `PreparedAgent.resume_interaction` path consumes the existing checkpoint instead of returning the same wait again.

A completed node is reconstructed only from one exact node-scoped `final_message`, one later `run_completed`, the matching visible `provider.turn_result` artifact and canonical provider accounting receipts for the same execution/root/owner/attempt/provider/model/iteration. The recovered public result contains the persisted final text plus canonical direct usage and is sealed through the same graph CAS/result-receipt path. Missing, duplicated, foreign, corrupt or partially terminal evidence is rejected; uncertain sends still become `outcome_unknown`. Recovery never calls `Agent.run`, never dispatches a tool and never invents a new attempt.

Red-before-green coverage now includes the original first-approval, second-approval and completed-terminal crash windows. Additional negative tests change a recovered approval checkpoint to a foreign node attempt and duplicate a terminal `final_message`; both reject while the graph remains running, with zero cold provider/tool calls. The completed-node test also verifies the reconstructed message, provider/model/profile and canonical total usage before continuing the downstream node.

With the same native provider output `  {"answer":"processed"}\n`, normal execution saves that exact string while cold recovery saves `{"answer":"processed"}`. A downstream Code/binding consumer of `message` therefore sees different data depending on a crash. Preserve the normal raw-output contract during recovery; validating against the normalized terminal event must not replace the actual node value. Cover leading/trailing whitespace, whitespace-only and ordinary text output parity.

### CP11-R2/R3 correction — locally fixed, ready for re-acceptance (2026-09-27)

CP11-R2 now verifies the interaction producer's actual persisted prompt manifest instead of synthesizing a system message unconditionally. Recovery derives the exact system/developer manifest from the integrity-checked checkpoint transcript, verifies the request subject digest against it, and for a nonempty configured prompt additionally requires the first manifest entry to be the exact Agent system instruction. An empty prompt therefore matches the producer's empty manifest, while nonempty prompt, execution/session, node attempt, checkpoint, request, provider and model identities remain fail-closed.

CP11-R3 now reconstructs the normal node value from the verified provider result envelope's last assistant message. The normalized `final_text` and semantic `final_message` remain independent terminal-consistency checks; neither replaces the raw assistant content. Leading/trailing whitespace and whitespace-only outputs now round-trip identically across normal completion and completed-node recovery. The raw value remains subject to the same later JSON/schema mapping as a normal node result.

Permanent repository tests cover ordinary and pre-graph-wait crash recovery with an empty prompt, plus exact raw-message parity for padded JSON and whitespace-only output. ### CP11-R2/R3 independent re-acceptance — FAIL, CP11-R4 / P2 (2026-09-27)

The original empty-prompt and single-message raw-output probes pass on a fresh standalone run: **5 passed in 8.09s**, including completed approval-resume recovery and uncertain-send non-repetition. The retained core tests also cover first/second approval gaps, foreign checkpoints and duplicate terminal receipts. These results validate the original R1–R3 scenarios; they do not establish general terminal-output parity.

**CP11-R4 / P2 — multi-message native completions cannot recover.** `unchain/src/unchain/skeleton/kernel_runtime.py:314–318` equates three different producer contracts: OpenAI's `ModelTurnResult.final_text` concatenates all text messages (`providers/openai.py:338`); `final_message` uses the last nonblank assistant text from the transcript (`kernel/lifecycle_events.py:14–21`); the normal Skeleton result preserves the last assistant content including whitespace (`kernel_runtime.py:474`). Those values need not be equal for a valid completion.

An independent native-response probe uses a schema-valid plain Agent (`json_schema: null`, result binding to built-in `message`), the real provider adapter and SQLite journal, and two assistant message items in one final response. With a preamble followed by `  Final answer.\n`, the ordinary execution completes through End and preserves the final raw string. A process loss immediately before `_save_completion` followed by a fresh store/handler makes `recover()` raise `ValueError: Kernel final provider result changed terminal content` at line 315; the graph remains running despite complete durable provider/final/terminal evidence. A preamble followed by a whitespace-only last message also completes normally but recovery fails the separate raw-message comparison at line 317. Both are reproducible (**2 failed in 7.23s**). This is one mismatch in terminal reconstruction, not two independent findings.

Correction criterion: reconstruct and validate each receipt according to its actual producer semantics while preserving the normal last-assistant node value. Do not require concatenated provider text to equal the last message, and do not require a whitespace-only last message to equal the semantic event's last nonblank text. Keep attempt/route/receipt/digest/accounting checks, reject incomplete or contradictory evidence, and perform no provider resend. Retain normal-versus-cold parity tests for multiple messages with both a nonblank and a whitespace-only last message, plus the existing single-message, approval and corruption negatives.

- Paired PuPu host/End/legacy graph/Flask interject tests: **38 passed in 36.46s**.
- Existing independent acceptance probes, fresh standalone retry: **5 passed in 8.09s**.
- New terminal parity assertions: **2 failed in 7.23s**, with successful ordinary completion asserted before each cold recovery failure.
- Both tracked whitespace checks pass. Full-repository and frontend counts from the implementation handoff were not rerun and remain historical.

### CP-12 implementation handoff — Tool Call, Skill Read, and approval (2026-09-27)

The owner explicitly authorized CP-12 while CP-11's required live-provider evidence remains **NOT_RUN**. That outstanding CP-11 work stays visible and is not represented as accepted or deferred. CP-12 introduces deterministic Workflow Tool Call and Skill Read only; it does not add Hook behavior, Code execution, storage/routes/UI publication, model execution, or a production sidecar rollout.

**Frozen node contracts.** A Tool Call node has a closed `config` with `execution` and `tool`; `tool` is the exact closed record `{name, on_error, approval_timeout_ms}`. `name` is nonempty, `on_error` is exactly `stop` or `continue`, and `approval_timeout_ms` is a bounded positive integer. Its already-resolved `execution.inputs` form the tool's argument object, so parameter values use the existing explicit input-binding contract rather than a second interpolation language. A Skill Read node has closed `config` with `execution` and `skill`; `skill` is the exact identity `{source, source_id, name}`. It cannot resolve by name alone. Its output envelope is closed as `{content, name}`, with immutable identity/revision metadata retained in the durable raw receipt but not injected into downstream public outputs unless explicitly bound.

**Tool host and sequence.** `SkeletonToolRuntime` receives an existing `Toolkit`, a secret-free graph/run identity and an injected clock. It publishes detached `Toolkit.to_json()` parameter schemas for the UI and calls the existing `prepare_tool_confirmation` / `execute_confirmable_tool_call` path; it does not reimplement tool parsing, policy, confirmation resolution, or tool invocation. A required confirmation becomes a closed, persisted `InteractionRequest` envelope containing only the rendered confirmation request and digests/identities, never credentials or raw secret-bearing host configuration. The runner exposes a Tool-specific resume entry that checks the current wait ID and node invocation, normalizes the response through the existing interaction contract, recomputes and compares the approval request before dispatch, then invokes the formal executor once. Duplicate/stale/forged approvals fail before execution. A timed-out wait can be explicitly expired before dispatch; cancellation uses the existing runner cancellation. If the process is lost after intent and before a durable raw result, normal cold recovery remains `outcome_unknown`; no automatic tool retry is permitted. `on_error: stop` returns a durable non-unknown node failure for a known denial/error; `continue` returns the closed `{result, ok, error}` result envelope. A rejected/cancelled/unknown operation never appears as `ok: true`.

**Skill host and sequence.** `SkeletonSkillRuntime` receives an injected `SkillRegistry`-compatible reader. It resolves the full source/source-id/name identity at dispatch, reads its body once, and returns `{content, name}`. The raw result retains the resolved identity and revision inside the runner's durable artifact. Committed or `result_received` reads never touch the filesystem again, so later source edits affect only a new graph. A source/read failure before a durable raw result is not silently converted into a different skill or an empty body.

**Allowed implementation surface.** Add `src/unchain/skeleton/tool_runtime.py` and `tests/test_skeleton_tool_runtime.py`; amend the untracked Skeleton execution/runner exports and validation only as needed for the two closed node contracts, Tool wait resumption/expiry, and known-safe terminal failures. Tests use a real `Toolkit`, real `InteractionRequest` normalization and the real SQLite workflow journal. Do not modify the existing formal tool executor, context durable tool boundary, provider code, skill registry, legacy Recipe bridge, or PuPu sidecar routes. The public host interface is intentionally usable by #352 but persistent PuPu resource routing remains CP-14.

**Required evidence.** Prove the same confirmable Tool follows its existing policy through the node path; schema list is detached; approve/reject/duplicate/stale/forged/expired approval, two sequential approvals, cancel, tool error policies, success-then-loss cold recovery, and no-receipt cold recovery are all covered. Prove Skill source identity mismatch fails, a committed read is unchanged after source mutation/cold recovery, and a new graph rereads. Run without model client construction, credential access or provider imports where the new explicit modules are imported. Record BC-002/006/007/011, AC-002/006/007/012 and SEQ-004/005 evidence; record actual `NOT_RUN` for no sidecar/UI/deployed-artifact pair. Stop after the CP-12 implementation and development review; no CP-13, commit, push, PR, rollout or closure.

### CP-12 implementation result — ready for owner acceptance (2026-09-27)

CP-12 implements the two deterministic Workflow hosts in the isolated Unchain clone. `SkeletonToolRuntime` validates the closed Tool Call contract, publishes a detached toolkit schema, reuses the established `prepare_tool_confirmation` and `execute_confirmable_tool_call` path, and persists the formal approval request plus a first and only first receipt before dispatch. `resume_tool_interaction` rejects stale, duplicate, forged, policy-changed and expired responses before invoking the tool. It leaves post-intent loss as `outcome_unknown`, never auto-retries a side effect, and commits the known closed envelope `{result, ok, error}` according to `on_error: stop|continue`. `SkeletonSkillRuntime` resolves only the complete `{source, source_id, name}` identity; committed output exposes only `{content, name}`, while the durable raw receipt retains source identity and revision.

The durable wait protocol now includes a nullable `receipt_ref`. The only permitted `waiting → waiting` progress is appending that receipt exactly once; a duplicate no-op is rejected. The runner may then advance the same persisted wait to `running` and dispatch through the existing executor. This supplies the required durable sequence without changing the existing formal executor, provider code, Context tool boundary, skill registry, legacy Recipe bridge, PuPu route, or production sidecar.

## CP-13 formal implementation handoff (2026-09-28)

**Ticket and workspace.** [#356](https://github.com/haoxiang-xu/PuPu/issues/356), branch `codex/ticket-356-skeleton-runtime`, PuPu base `f9c870e6075262975b9b6fa20ef572b49d39064d`, Unchain base `0b2ad609fb28577fbea118382666121bb66e2cd1`. Work remains uncommitted in `/Users/red/Desktop/GITRepo/pupu-356` and `/Users/red/Desktop/GITRepo/unchain-356`. CP-12 is accepted; CP-11 live-provider evidence remains `NOT_RUN` and is not repaired or reclassified here.

**Goal and flow.** CP-13 gives `code` nodes one official, provider-free Python host. A prepared Skeleton carries a closed `config.code` reference to a relative source file and a local runtime-profile ID. The host resolves both through explicit local adapters, snapshots the source into a private staging directory, fingerprints the source and exact runtime command, launches without a shell, sends strict JSON input/context over a private request file, captures stdout/stderr as bounded logs, and accepts the result only from a separate versioned receipt file whose graph/node/invocation and fingerprints match. The user function is exactly `main(input, context)` and must return a JSON object satisfying the optional result schema. The durable raw receipt retains provenance and logs while output mappings see only the user result.

**Decisions frozen for this checkpoint.** The portable Skeleton stores `environment_profile_id`, never an absolute interpreter path. `FileCodeSourceResolver` confines relative paths to one declared root and reads a regular source file once; the staged copy is the executed artifact. `PythonEnvironmentProfile` is host-supplied and has exactly two launch forms: `venv` uses one absolute Python executable with `-I -c`; `bundled` uses one absolute frozen-host command prefix implementing the same worker protocol. Neither form activates a shell, installs dependencies, rewrites the environment, blocks network/SDK access, or claims sandboxing. Timeout or a worker/process crash before an authenticated receipt is `outcome_unknown`; a valid worker receipt reporting an invalid return/schema is a known failed node. Cancellation revokes durable ownership, the polling host kills the process group, and no late result can publish. Result and each log stream have independent byte ceilings.

The existing custom-handler form (`config.execution` only) remains readable for CP-8/CP-10 compatibility; constructing the official `SkeletonCodeRuntime` requires the new closed `config.code`. This transitional compatibility does not let the official host infer source or environment. The separate Python-project exporter ticket is out of scope. PuPu UI/profile persistence and deployed wheel/sidecar pairing remain CP-14. CP-13 may add only the minimal frozen-sidecar worker entry needed to prove that a supported packaged candidate can run the same protocol; it does not add an Electron route.

**Files and symbols.** Add `src/unchain/skeleton/code_runtime.py` and a small worker entrypoint module; export `SkeletonCodeRuntime`, `PythonEnvironmentProfile`, and `FileCodeSourceResolver` lazily. Extend `execution._validate_all` only for present `config.code`, and extend `DurableWorkflowRunner` only to bind the official handler to the store plan and to unwrap its private durable receipt. Add focused `tests/test_skeleton_code_runtime.py` fixtures/probes. If packaged-host proof needs a PuPu flag, limit changes to the existing sidecar entrypoint plus its mirrored tests/build inclusion. Do not touch provider, KernelLoop, AgentBuilder, legacy Recipe, workflow-call, Hook, UI, resource store, or export-project behavior.

**Boundary record.** BC-002/003/006/012; AC-002/003/006/012/014; SEQ-004/011. Admission is CLOSED for Code config and worker receipts, VERSIONED by their schema strings, and OPEN only inside the declared user result governed by its result schema. Source bytes, runtime command, resolved input and invocation identity are fixed before launch. An authenticated success/error receipt precedes durable publication; absent or unauthenticated receipts never authorize retry. Real installed-candidate evidence must identify the exact candidate and executable digest; a development `.venv` can prove the venv path but cannot satisfy bundled evidence.

**Impact and suitability.** GitNexus cannot resolve the untracked CP1–CP12 Skeleton symbols and returns `UNKNOWN`; text inspection finds 49 `DurableWorkflowRunner` construction sites, all in the new Skeleton tests/current package, so tests must carry the compatibility proof. The tracked process supervisor is a useful process-tree reference but has different detached-job semantics and is not edited. This slice is **not suitable for weaker-model delegation**: protocol authentication, cancellation races, process-tree cleanup, staged-artifact identity, frozen-host compatibility and outcome-unknown classification require coupled judgment. The active strong agent implements and reviews it directly.

**Required red/green evidence and stop.** Red tests must first prove the official host is absent. Green coverage must include real venv Start→Code→End and mixed Tool/Skill→Code flow, standalone import/prepare/run with no model credentials, stdout log pollution, valid result-schema enforcement, non-JSON return, wrong identity, oversized result/logs, missing/corrupt runtime, missing dependency, crash/outcome-unknown, timeout, concurrent cancellation with no surviving child, source mutation after staging, staging-copy mutation isolation, path escape/symlink/permission/content checks, environment/code fingerprint persistence, and official-plan mismatch before launch. A supported packaged/frozen candidate must run the same worker protocol or the checkpoint reports that acceptance item `NOT_RUN`/blocked; it may not substitute the development interpreter. Run focused tests, full Skeleton/Context regression, paired PuPu boundary tests, compilation and change-scope checks. Stop after CP-13 evidence; do not begin CP-14, commit, push, open a PR, roll out, or close #356.

## CP-13 implementation checkpoint (2026-09-28)

**Delivered.** Unchain now has an official `SkeletonCodeRuntime`, a host-local `PythonEnvironmentProfile`, a confined `FileCodeSourceResolver`, and a public immutable `CodeSourceSnapshot`. A Code node binds a closed relative-file/profile/timeout/result/log/schema contract. The runtime resolves and fingerprints source plus the exact runtime command before execution; rechecks runtime content before writing a durable effect intent; copies source into a private per-call directory; launches with an argv array and no shell; supplies only detached JSON input and declared identity/work-directory context; drains stdout/stderr concurrently into independent bounded buffers; and reads results solely from a separate versioned identity-bound receipt. The durable raw result retains code/runtime SHA-256 and logs, while normal bindings receive only the user result.

The worker calls exactly `main(input, context)`. A returned non-object, non-JSON value, result over the configured byte ceiling, or result-schema mismatch becomes a persisted known failure and is not retried. A timeout, missing dependency, process crash, missing/malformed receipt or wrong receipt identity becomes `outcome_unknown`, preserving the possibility of external side effects. Durable cancellation revokes ownership while the host is polling, kills the process group and rejects late publication. Timeout and cancel tests each launch a real descendant and prove it is gone afterward. The worker does not activate a shell, install dependencies, block network/SDK imports, or claim sandboxing.

Compatibility remains explicit: old CP-8/CP-10 custom Code handlers can still use execution-only config, but a present `config.code` is CLOSED for every runner and the official host requires it. Official Code handlers must match the store's complete fixed plan, including cold construction. Code works in both Workflow and Agent plans. The existing three-provider mixed Agent test now finishes through a real Python subprocess rather than a lambda. A separate Skill Read → real Code → End test proves explicit host composition. No provider, AgentBuilder, legacy Recipe, UI, workflow-call, Hook, resource-store, Electron route or Python-project-export implementation was added.

## CP-13 correction implementation (2026-09-28)

R1–R5 are corrected in the isolated CP13 trees and await independent re-acceptance. The durable execution state now carries an optional, CLOSED `code_bindings` envelope for official Code hosts. At execution creation, each Code source is stored as a verified immutable artifact and its source digest, profile ID/kind/exact command/fingerprint and declared attachment mappings are fixed atomically with the execution record. A fresh runner restores these bindings from the journal; it reads the stored source only when Code must execute, compares the currently resolved profile with the frozen identity, and therefore can reach End from an existing result receipt even after the original source file has disappeared. Legacy execution records without this envelope remain readable, but cannot silently dispatch an official Code host.

The `code` configuration now admits an optional CLOSED `artifacts` array of `{name, attachment}` mappings. Creation verifies each granted attachment; execution verifies its durable ref, stages an invocation-private file copy, and exposes only the mapped path/digest/media metadata through `context.artifacts`. The user code never receives a journal handle or shared artifact inode. This completes the CP13 Code portion of BC-012 / AC-014; broader Start/provider/child artifact combinations remain later CP14/CP17 work.

The standard-library worker now validates the full bounded JSON tree before encoding, so it rejects tuples, non-string object keys, non-finite numbers and unsupported Python objects instead of accepting serializer-normalized values. The host performs a final ownership check immediately before `Popen`. Its POSIX cleanup now owns the private process group independently of whether the worker leader has already exited, covering normal completion, timeout and cancellation; receipt handling begins only after that cleanup.

**Correction evidence.** New permanent tests cover fresh-runner source/profile recovery, completion after source deletion with a saved receipt, scoped artifact staging/copy isolation, strict JSON rejection, SIGTERM-resistant descendants after success and timeout, and cancellation observed at the launch boundary. Focused Code plus the retained independent acceptance probes: **31 passed, 1 skipped**. Durable/store/kernel/mixed regression: **69 passed**. The same Code/mixed/durable group using PuPu's bundled Python: **63 passed**. Full Skeleton suite with the bundled interpreter: **385 passed in 159.91s**. Compilation and whitespace checks passed. Retained reports: `/Users/red/.codex/artifacts/pupu-356/cp13-acceptance/correction-focused.xml`, `correction-regression.xml`, `correction-bundled.xml`, and `correction-skeleton-full.xml`.

**Boundary status.** BC-003/006 now use the durable producer (`DurableWorkflowRunner.create`) and strict consumer (`validate_execution_state` / `WorkflowExecutionStore` / `SkeletonCodeRuntime`) rather than a process-local binding. BC-012 maps durable attachment artifact → verified private Code file with positive and source-deletion/local-mutation negative evidence. SEQ-011 covers create → fixed bindings → pause/cold runner → Code or saved-receipt End, plus cancellation before dispatch. GitNexus still returns UNKNOWN for untracked Skeleton symbols; `detect-changes` reports HIGH only for 25 earlier tracked Context symbols and no CP13 symbol, which is not an all-clear. CP11 live-provider and installed/deployed artifact-pair evidence remain NOT_RUN. No commit, push, PR, rollout, ticket closure or CP14 work has occurred. This is an implementation correction, not acceptance; CP13 remains at the owner-requested independent acceptance gate.

### CP13-R6–R9 correction — locally fixed, ready for re-acceptance (2026-09-28)

R6–R9 are corrected in the isolated CP13 tree. The official Code host no longer keeps one mutable graph-wide binding. `DurableWorkflowRunner` binds only the pending Code node immediately before its preflight, keyed by the durable `node_attempt_id`; the handler releases that entry after the process attempt. A shared handler can therefore serve distinct graph attempts without source/profile crossover, while a legacy record without `code_bindings` has no matching attempt entry and fails closed.

Artifact enumeration now sorts attachment and Code-node keys before constructing the journal resource list, matching canonical state encoding on both write and cold decode. The strict durable consumer also compares a present `code_bindings` envelope with the fixed plan: its node set, profile ID and ordered `{name, attachment}` mapping must exactly match each declared `config.code`. Missing, extra and remapped bindings reject before execution. Code artifact names are type-checked before path/duplicate checks, so arrays and objects return a structured `SkeletonValidationError` instead of a raw `TypeError`.

## CP-14 implementation checkpoint — ready for independent acceptance (2026-09-28)

**Delivered.** PuPu now owns a transactional SQLite Skeleton resource store at `~/.pupu/skeletons/resources.sqlite3`, with stable Agent/Workflow IDs, incomplete draft save plus diagnostics, opaque edit-token CAS, strict Run/Publish preflight, immutable Workflow revisions and fixed transitive dependency closures. Rename preserves identity; duplicate requires a new identity; import is idempotent only for the same ID and content. Archive blocks new use while retained revisions stay readable; physical deletion rejects retained revisions, incoming references and legacy aliases. Agent resources remain unversioned for users, while every preflight returns one exact document/digest/token snapshot for the caller to freeze at run creation.

The official Unchain Workflow Call runtime accepts only a fixed `{workflow_id,revision_id}` reference. It derives deterministic child graph/attempt identities, shares the durable journal without sharing the child call-stack frame, persists the parent `kind=child` wait, returns only child End outputs, and supports nested cold resume and recursive cancellation. Publish rejects missing, archived, wrong-kind, cyclic, recursive, over-depth and over-count dependency graphs before creating the revision. Publishing a later revision does not alter a child already fixed to the earlier revision.

Legacy recipe, legacy Skeleton template and Soul migration use explicit discovered→written→validated→active records with exact source hashes; original files remain untouched. Legacy scope/name resolution never falls back to Default for an explicit bad selection. Default is used only for an omitted selection and must be unique. Real startup exposed that the existing seeders create both `Explore.recipe` and `Explore.skeleton`; both exact built-in seeds now map to one canonical Explore Agent while retaining independent `user:agent_recipes` and `user:subagents` aliases. This collapse requires byte-parsed equality with both built-in definitions, so user-authored same-name files remain separate and retain normal ambiguity/conflict handling. A fresh sidecar proved the live list contains exactly Default and one Explore, and both legacy scopes resolve to the same stable ID.

PuPu also stores local Python environment profiles used by Code nodes. The read-only built-in profile resolves PuPu's managed runtime; a user profile requires an absolute executable, probes CPython ≥3.10 without a shell, fingerprints the exact executable and uses CAS. Draft preflight and Workflow publish reject a missing, changed or unavailable profile; deletion rejects profiles retained by drafts or revisions. A saved venv profile was exercised through a published Workflow and the real Python Code host.

**Product boundary.** Authenticated Flask routes cover list/get/save/diagnose/import/preflight/publish/version/rename/duplicate/archive/restore/delete/migrate/resolve and Python profile list/save/probe/delete. Request objects are CLOSED and failures use stable `code + path + message`. Electron exposes one fixed IPC command with an action allowlist; the main service reconstructs method, path and body instead of accepting caller-supplied transport fields. Preload and renderer wrappers expose only those fixed actions. The existing recipe API remains available during migration. The sidecar startup keeps recipe seeding and Skeleton migration in separate failure domains and reports isolated bad-source failures without preventing later migrations or startup.

### CP14-R1–R7 correction — locally fixed, ready for re-acceptance (2026-09-28)

R1–R3 are corrected in the official Workflow Call runtime. Parent cancellation now commits the parent cancellation fence before propagating to a deterministically identified child, including a child that is still running and has not emitted a parent wait receipt. Child creation rechecks parent ownership after creating its durable record and cancels itself if that ownership was lost. Cold parent recovery derives the exact child graph/attempt identity and consumes a completed result, a durable wait, or an already received result without replaying completed child work. Official Code, Tool and Skill handlers expose per-plan rebinding, so each nested Workflow gets a handler bound to its own fixed plan while generic handlers remain reusable. The original real-Python cancellation, process-death recovery and root→middle→leaf official-Code probes now pass.

R4 keeps strict Agent admission intact. Legacy Default/Explore resources remain inert compatibility drafts until the PuPu host supplies one explicit, fully validated Kernel configuration at Run preflight. Unchain then creates a detached executable Agent snapshot with closed `execution + kernel` configs, explicit Start bindings, fixed `message/context` Agent outputs and End-only root outputs. Legacy text/builtin prompt and max-iteration behavior are projected onto the selected profile; an explicit legacy model must match the selected model. No API key or provider default is inferred from the file. The Electron service, preload and renderer API carry only this closed `legacy_kernel` field. Both real seeds reject an omitted profile, accept the explicit profile, pass `prepare_agent_graph`, execute through an Agent handler and commit End outputs. The two original acceptance assertions that expected no-argument legacy preflight were intentionally replaced by this stricter host-resolution contract; the corrected external test is retained at `/Users/red/.codex/artifacts/pupu-356/cp14-correction/test_legacy_agent_preflight.py`.

R6 returns an already active migration from its recorded provenance/alias before comparing the user-editable target name or digest, so rename/edit after activation survives a rescan while the original legacy alias still resolves the same stable ID. R7 type-admits `source_kind` and `stop_after_stage` before variant membership; actual Flask requests with arrays/objects now return structured 4xx errors and field paths.

### CP14-R8–R13 correction — locally fixed, ready for re-acceptance (2026-09-28)

R8 makes cancellation propagation retryable after the parent has durably entered `cancelled`: a fresh `cancel()` re-identifies and cancels the active child instead of returning before descendant propagation. R9 converts a durably known `child_failed` or `child_cancelled` recovery result into a permitted, persisted parent failure; an unknown child outcome remains `outcome_unknown`, and completed work is not replayed.

R10 now reads the root legacy recipe for every Kernel node. Root `max_iterations` applies to every node, provider-qualified root model inheritance is checked per node, and a single supplied host profile fails closed when a legacy graph requires a different node model instead of silently substituting it. R11 translates legacy `{{#node.output#}}` prompt references into explicit Skeleton input bindings (`output` → `message`) and renders those bound values before context budgeting, provider dispatch and durable interaction identity checks.

R12 preserves the exact saved venv invocation path while its fingerprint still records the resolved executable artifact. A standard symlinked venv therefore retains its own `sys.prefix` and site-packages. R13 validates Python environment profiles in every published Workflow document reached through the fixed transitive closure and returns the dependency/node path before root execution begins.
